# Task classifications, counts and source index

The source collection contains 3,283 post records. The article’s findings for seven agents use 757 selected records. Posts can appear in more than one task category; category counts must not be added as if they were distinct posts. Counts describe this sample, not success rates or a ranking.

Each section preserves its classification, stated post/author counts, record IDs and original source links. This English index lists classifications rather than reproducing the research notes; open a linked post for the original report. The additional-reference sections cover the broader collection and are not the denominator of the 757-record task-feedback chart.
